# claudart: which test guards which line

> Generated by `dart run tool/proof_audit/run.dart --mutants=... ` and `ledger.dart` from the mutant rows of `proofs.jsonl`. A mutant is caught at tier 1 by the file's mirrored test, at tier 2 only by the rest of the suite (the mirrored test is weak or missing), or it survives: no test guards that line.

## Tests that kill exactly the same mutants

No mutant tells these tests apart. They may assert different things about the same logic (then keep both and say so) or the same thing twice (then merge, or link one to the other).

- 9 mutants: SessionLogger entries: optional fields appear only when passed the same fingerprint is one entry with a growing count; SessionLogger same fingerprint increments count, not adds new entry; SessionLogger with concurrent writers (real processes) one fingerprint logged from several processes keeps its full count
- 9 mutants: parseEditFiles content containing the closing tag mid-line is kept intact; parseEditFiles two files, first containing the closing tag, both parse
- 8 mutants: link — sensitivity flags and end of input --no-sensitive turns it OFF without asking; link — sensitivity flags and end of input re-linking an existing project --no-sensitive flips an ON project to OFF without asking
- 8 mutants: link — .gitignore edge cases appends to existing .gitignore without clobbering; link — new project registration adds .claude to .gitignore when not present
- 8 mutants: teardown — pre-population accepts pre-populated hotFiles when user returns null; teardown — skills.md appends hot path entry for changed file
- 7 mutants: runDebug — the verification command debug has not returned while the close is still running; runDebug — the verification command headless, verified: the session closes itself; runDebug — the verification command headless: debug returns only after the close has finished
- 7 mutants: tagOrNullIgnoreCase lower-case tag matches upper-case lookup; tagOrNullIgnoreCase mismatched open/close case still matches; tagOrNullIgnoreCase mixed-case tag matches
- 7 mutants: link — sensitivity flags and end of input --sensitive turns it ON without asking; link — sensitivity is reconciled once and recorded on the entry --sensitive records it; the settings carry no debug env
- 7 mutants: runSuggest — approved findings approving moves suggestInvestigating to readyForDebug and keeps the root cause; runSuggest — approved findings logs the transition it applied: command, from, event, to; runSuggest — resuming from needs-suggest a session that is not blocked logs no suggestResumed; runSuggest — resuming from needs-suggest logs suggestResumed, then saveConfirmedReady on approval; and 2 more
- 7 mutants: teardown — pre-population no hotFiles default when changedFiles is blank; teardown — pre-population uses override when user types a different hotFiles value
- 7 mutants: detectGitContext parses root and branch from a successful rev-parse; detectGitContext workingDirectory asks git in the given directory, so another project's branch can be read from here
- 6 mutants: runHook a Dart edit outside a ready session advises, with the reason; runHook in a strict workspace the same edit is denied
- 6 mutants: runSuggest — status gate follows HandoffStatus.after debugInProgress: runs and locks readyForDebug; runSuggest — status gate follows HandoffStatus.after readyForDebug: runs and locks readyForDebug
- 6 mutants: teardown — pre-population accepts pre-populated pattern when user returns null; teardown — pre-population no pattern default when root cause is blank; teardown — pre-population uses override when user types a different pattern; teardown — skills.md appends root cause pattern entry; and 2 more
- 6 mutants: teardown — headless closes only on verified evidence a finished fix with no disposition recorded: saves a reminder; teardown — headless closes only on verified evidence a fix applied and analyzed, never verified: saves a reminder; teardown — headless closes only on verified evidence a handoff suggest confirmed, debug skipped: saves a reminder
- 5 mutants: link — dependency-config codegen does NOT recompile by default, even when bin/<name>.dart exists and content changed; link — dependency-config codegen regenerates lib/generated/dependency_config.g.dart from pubspec.yaml; link — dependency-config codegen usesDartrix is false when pubspec.yaml has no dartrix dependency
- 5 mutants: runHook a blank handoff counts as no session: advises; runHook an unreadable workspace.json still advises, and the failure is logged by its type, not dropped
- 5 mutants: runDebug — the root cause in the prompt "Found it." is sent to the model; runDebug — the root cause in the prompt "_Not determined._" is not sent to the model; runDebug — the root cause in the prompt "_Not yet determined._" is not sent to the model
- 5 mutants: wrappedLineCount narrow terminal; wrappedLineCount two full lines
- 5 mutants: wrappedLineCount one char past width; wrappedLineCount start of third line
- 5 mutants: link — re-linking existing project can update sensitivityMode on re-link; link — sensitivity flags and end of input answering yes and no at the prompt behaves as before; link — sensitivity mode sensitivityMode false when user declines
- 5 mutants: tagOrNullIgnoreCase preserves slice indices through non-ASCII non-ASCII inside tag content is preserved verbatim; tagOrNullIgnoreCase preserves slice indices through non-ASCII surrogate pair in tag content is preserved; tagOrNullIgnoreCase upper-case tag matches upper-case lookup
- 5 mutants: link — sensitivity flags and end of input re-linking an existing project declining to change keeps the current setting; link — sensitivity flags and end of input re-linking an existing project end of input at "Enable sensitivity mode?" aborts and keeps the setting
- 5 mutants: PipelineExecutor — per-step model record events — one stream of every event a completed step carries the models it ran on; PipelineExecutor — per-step model record events — one stream of every event run publishes every event, in order; PipelineExecutor — per-step model record events — one stream of every event runFuture publishes the same events
